namespace = sino_event
sino_event.1 = {
	#东亚病夫
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/unspecific_naval_battle.bk2"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/military_parade"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"
	title = sino_event.1.t
	desc = sino_event.105.d
	flavor = sino_event.105.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	minor_left_icon = c:CHI
	minor_right_icon = c:JAP
	trigger = {
	}
	immediate = {
		s:STATE_FORMOSA = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:CHI
					owner ?= {
						OR = {
							c:CHI ?= this
							is_subject_of = c:CHI
						}
					}
				}
				set_state_owner = c:JAP
			}
		}
		c:CHI ?= {
			save_scope_as = chi_scope
		}
		c:JAP ?= {
			save_scope_as = jap_scope
			add_treasury = 900000
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.1.a
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = dongyabingfu_modifier
			months = 240
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = sub_china_warlord_explosion_tt
			change_variable = {
				name = china_warlord_explosion
				add = -1
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = spend_money_to_japan_tt			#日本那边结算已经赔钱了，这个通告事件不用再额外赔钱
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = china_fail_tt			#增加救亡图存的进度条
			je:je_china_19th_century = {
				add_progress = {
					value = 1
					name = china_reformist_progress_bar
				}
			}
		}
		trigger_event = {
			id = sino_event.8
			months = 24
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						country_rank = rank_value:great_power
						country_rank = rank_value:super_power
						country_rank = rank_value:major_power
					}
					NOT = {
						c:CHI ?= this
					}
					NOT = {
						c:JAP ?= this
					}
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = sino_event.3
					days = {
						3
						30
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sino_event.2 = {
	#甲午胜利
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	gui_window = event_window_widescreen_classic
	event_image = {
		video = "chi2"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/military_parade"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/zhuri.dds"
	title = sino_event.2.t
	desc = sino_event.2.d
	flavor = sino_event.2.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	minor_left_icon = c:CHI
	minor_right_icon = c:JAP
	trigger = {
	}
	immediate = {
		c:JAP ?= {
			save_scope_as = jap_scope
		}
		c:CHI ?= {
			save_scope_as = chi_scope
		}
		c:KOR ?= {
			save_scope_as = kor_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.2.a
		default_option = yes
		add_loyalists = {
			value = 0.1
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = has_won_one_race_tt
		}
		je:je_china_and_japan = {
			add_progress = {
				value = 1
				name = china_japan_progress_bar
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = china_win2
			months = 120
		}
	}
}

		}
	}

sino_event.4 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/europenorthamerica_capitalists_meeting.bk2"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/military_parade"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/Twelve_Symbols_national_emblem_of_China.png"
	title = sino_event.4.t
	desc = sino_event.4.d
	flavor = sino_event.4.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	trigger = {
	}
	immediate = {
		c:GBR ?= {
			save_scope_as = gbr_scope
		}
		c:FRA ?= {
			save_scope_as = fra_scope
		}
		c:BIC ?= {
			save_scope_as = bic_scope
		}
		c:CHI ?= {
			save_scope_as = chi_scope
		}
		g:opium = {
			save_scope_as = opium_scope
		}
		g:tea = {
			save_scope_as = tea_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.4.a
		default_option = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:FRA
				exists = c:GBR
				c:FRA ?= {
					is_player = no
				}
			}
			c:FRA ?= {
				set_secret_goal = {
					country = c:GBR
					secret_goal = befriend
				}
				every_scope_treaty = {
					limit = {
						binds = c:TUR
						any_scope_article = {
							has_type = alliance
						}
					}
					withdraw = {
						country = root
					}
				}
				change_relations = {
					country = c:CHI
					value = -20
				}
			}
		}
		c:GBR ?= {
			trigger_event = {
				id = clever_dsk.193
				days = 10
			}
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						country_rank = rank_value:great_power
						country_rank = rank_value:super_power
						country_rank = rank_value:major_power
					}
					NOT = {
						c:CHI ?= this
					}
					NOT = {
						c:FRA ?= this
					}
					NOT = {
						c:GBR ?= this
					}
					NOT = {
						c:USA ?= this
					}
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = sino_event.5
					days = {
						3
						15
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
}

sino_event.8 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/europenorthamerica_political_extremism.bk2"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/military_parade"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"
	title = sino_event.8.t
	desc = sino_event.8.d
	flavor = sino_event.8.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	trigger = {
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.8.a
		default_option = yes
		ig:ig_intelligentsia = {
			remove_ideology = ideology_anti_slavery
			add_ideology = ideology_china_reformer
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = bianfa_modifier
			years = 2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc_feature = voice_of_the_people_content
				empty_agitator_slots >= 1
			}
			create_character = {
				template = ecchi_chi_kang_youwei_character_template
				is_agitator = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

sino_event.10 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	event_image = {
		video = "gfx/event_pictures/europenorthamerica_capitalists_meeting.bk2"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/military_parade"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/japan.dds"
	title = sino_event.10.t
	desc = sino_event.10.d
	flavor = sino_event.10.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	minor_left_icon = c:CHI
	minor_right_icon = c:JAP
	trigger = {
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = china_no_war_with_japan_var
		c:CHI ?= {
			save_scope_as = chi_scope
		}
		c:JAP ?= {
			set_secret_goal = {
				country = ROOT
				secret_goal = befriend
			}
			save_scope_as = jap_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.10.a
		default_option = yes
		create_bidirectional_truce = {
			country = c:JAP
			months = 1200
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:JAP
			value = 300
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_diplomatic_pact = {
						who = c:JAP
						type = qwf_interest_alliance
					}
				}
			}
			create_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = c:JAP
				type = qwf_interest_alliance
			}
		}
	}
}

					add_modifier = agb_modifier

			}
		}
	}

			multiplier = 10
		}

			add_claim = c:CHI
		}
		s:STATE_TIANSHAN = {
			add_claim = c:CHI
		}
		c:XIN ?= {

				name = conquer_war
				type = dp_conquer_state
				escalation = 76
				target_state = s:STATE_DZUNGARIA.region_state:CHI
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:CHI
					type = return_state
					target_state = s:STATE_TIANSHAN.region_state:XIN
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:CHI
					type = return_state
					target_state = s:STATE_KIRGHIZIA.region_state:XIN
				}
			}
		}

			}
		}
	}

				}

				is_historical = no

					ig_counts_as_marginal = no

#太平洋战争，战争进入全面阶段
sino_event.34 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:beautiful_state
	event_image = {
		video = "yamato"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"
	title = sino_event.34.t
	desc = sino_event.34.d
	flavor = sino_event.34.f
	minor_left_icon = c:USA
	minor_right_icon = c:JAP
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	duration = 3
	cooldown = {
		years = 100
	}
	trigger = {
		c:JAP ?= this
		c:JAP ?= {
			NOT = {
				has_war_with = c:USA
			}
		}
		c:USA ?= {
			relations:root < relations_threshold:amicable
		}
		OR = {
			scope:journal_entry = {
				"scripted_bar_progress(je_china_war_bar)" <= 700				# 进度超过2/3
			}
			scope:journal_entry = {
				"scripted_bar_progress(je_protect_china_war_bar)" >= 3				# 进度超过2/3
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_role_of_type = politician
				age > 18
				is_busy = no`
				is_ruler_of_own_country = no
				is_in_government = yes
				interest_group = {
					ig_counts_as_marginal = no
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = gaixi
		}
		random_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_HAWAIIAN_ISLANDS
			}
			save_scope_as = beautiful_state
		}
		c:JAP ?= {
			save_scope_as = jap_scope
		}
		c:GBR ?= {
			save_scope_as = gbr_scope
		}
		c:USA ?= {
			save_scope_as = usa_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.34.a
		default_option = yes
		set_global_variable = chi_usa_gbr_will_win_var
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = add_2_protect_china_war_bar_tt
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					has_journal_entry = je_protect_china
				}
				je:je_protect_china = {
					add_progress = {
						value = 2
						name = je_protect_china_war_bar
					}
				}
			}
		}
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_annex_amercia
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:USA
			value = -200
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:GBR
			value = -200
		}
		create_diplomatic_pact = {
			country = c:USA
			type = rivalry
		}
		create_diplomatic_pact = {
			country = c:GBR
			type = rivalry
		}
		c:USA ?= {
			change_relations = {
				country = c:CHI
				value = 200
			}
			set_secret_goal = {
				country = root
				secret_goal = conquer
			}
			create_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = root
				type = rivalry
			}
		}
		c:GBR ?= {
			change_relations = {
				country = c:CHI
				value = 200
			}
			set_secret_goal = {
				country = root
				secret_goal = conquer
			}
			create_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = root
				type = rivalry
			}
		}
		limit = {
			NOT = {
				owns_entire_state_region = STATE_MALAYA
			}
		}
		s:STATE_MALAYA = {
			add_claim = ROOT
		}

			add_claim = ROOT
		}

			add_claim = ROOT
		}

			}

			add_claim = ROOT

			add_claim = ROOT

#神佛分离
sino_event.35 = {
	type = country_event
	event_image = {
		video = "jap2"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/torii.png"
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	title = sino_event.35.t
	desc = sino_event.35.d
	flavor = sino_event.35.f
	trigger = {
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.35.a
		default_option = yes
		add_radicals = {
			religion = rel:mahayana
			value = 0.02
		}
		set_state_religion = rel:shinto
		activate_law = law_type:law_IJ_kami
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = IJ_kami_var
		}
		ig:ig_devout = {
			hidden_effect = {
				set_interest_group_name = ig_shinto_monks
			}
			remove_ideology = ideology_buddhist_moralist
			remove_ideology = ideology_mahayana_buddhist_moralist
			add_ideology = ideology_shinto_moralist
			set_ig_trait = ig_trait:ig_trait_haibutsu_kishaku
			set_ig_trait = ig_trait:ig_trait_secular_shrine_theory
			set_ig_trait = ig_trait:ig_trait_heavenly_sovereign
			leader ?= {
				change_character_religion = rel:shinto
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_dlc_feature = ep2_content
				has_variable = japan_restoration_complete
				has_variable = japan_emperor_restored
				NOT = {
					has_variable = shinbutsu_bunri_decision_taken
				}
			}
			add_journal_entry = {
				type = je_shinbutsu_bunri
			}
			show_as_tooltip = {
				every_scope_homeland_state = {
					convert_population = {
						target = rel:shinto
						value = 0.1
					}
				}
			}
			set_variable = shinbutsu_bunri_decision_taken
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.35.b
		add_loyalists = {
			religion = rel:mahayana
			value = 0.05
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = god_budda_peace
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

sino_event.41 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:crisis_scope_2
	minor_left_icon = c:JAP
	minor_right_icon = c:RUS
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/rus.png"
	title = sino_event.41.t
	desc = sino_event.41.d
	flavor = sino_event.41.f
	cooldown = {
		months = 360
	}
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	trigger = {
		c:RUS ?= {
			is_ai = yes
			relations:root < relations_threshold:amicable
			NOT = {
				has_truce_with = c:CHI
			}
		}
		AND = {
			has_state_in_state_region = STATE_SAKHALIN
			c:CHI ?= {
				NOT = {
					has_state_in_state_region = STATE_SAKHALIN
				}
			}
			has_truce_with = c:CHI
			c:CHI ?= {
				has_modifier = warispolitics_lost_war
			}
			c:JAP ?= {
				has_modifier = warispolitics_won_war
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		c:RUS ?= {
			save_scope_as = rus_scope
			ruler ?= {
				save_scope_as = crisis_scope_2
				set_variable = {
					name = ecchi_27_proud
					months = 12
				}
			}
		}
		random_state = {
			limit = {
				state_region = s:STATE_SAKHALIN
			}
			save_scope_as = beautiful_state
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.41.a
		default_option = yes
		c:RUS ?= {
			change_relations = {
				country = ROOT
				value = -100
			}
		}
		c:RUS ?= {
			set_secret_goal = {
				country = root
				secret_goal = conquer
			}
			create_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = c:JAP
				type = rivalry
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.41.b
		s:STATE_SAKHALIN = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:JAP
					owner ?= {
						OR = {
							c:JAP ?= this
							is_subject_of = c:JAP
						}
					}
				}
				set_state_owner = c:RUS
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:RUS
			value = 100
		}
		change_infamy = -10
	}
}

					devastation > 0.1
					is_incorporated = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = skirmish_state
			}
		}
		c:JAP ?= {
			save_scope_as = jap_scope
		}
		c:CHI ?= {
			save_scope_as = chi_scope
		}
		c:CHI ?= {
			random_scope_war = {
				save_scope_as = ongoing_acw
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.43.a
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = acw_special_events_skirmish_state.tt
			scope:ongoing_acw = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = c:JAP
					}
					add_war_war_support = {
						target = c:JAP
						value = 100
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						exists = c:CHI
					}
					add_war_war_support = {
						target = c:CHI
						value = 100
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sino_event.60 = {
	#甲申3\开化党
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	event_image = {
		video = "ep1_donghak_rebellion"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/waving_flag.dds"
	cooldown = {
		months = 3600
	}
	title = sino_event.107.t
	desc = sino_event.107.d
	flavor = sino_event.60.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	minor_left_icon = root
	minor_right_icon = c:KOR
	trigger = {
		c:KOR ?= {
			is_ai = yes
			is_subject_of = c:CHI
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		c:CHI ?= {
			save_scope_as = chi_scope
		}
		c:KOR ?= {
			save_scope_as = kor_scope
			activate_law = law_type:law_wealth_voting			#财产投票
			activate_law = law_type:law_laissez_faire			#自由放任
			activate_law = law_type:law_free_trade			#自由贸易
			activate_law = law_type:law_no_migration_controls			#无移民控制
		}
		c:JAP ?= {
			save_scope_as = jap_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.60.a
		default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = korean_historical_c_tt
		set_variable = {
			name = chi_jiashen_var
			years = 40
		}
		scope:jap_scope ?= {
			create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
				type = catalyst_event_negative
				target = c:CHI
			}
			set_secret_goal = {
				country = c:CHI
				secret_goal = conquer
			}
		}
		scope:kor_scope ?= {
			create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
				type = catalyst_event_negative
				target = c:CHI
			}
			set_secret_goal = {
				country = c:CHI
				secret_goal = antagonize
			}
			create_diplomatic_play = {
				name = kor_duli_war
				target_country = c:CHI
				type = dp_independence
				escalation = 25
			}
		}
	}
}

sino_event.70 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	gui_window = event_window_superevent_modern_full
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/soe_event_pictures/roc.dds"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/military_parade"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/roc.dds"
	title = sino_event.70.t
	desc = {
		first_valid = {
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = sino_event.70.d.a
				trigger = {
					scope:journal_entry = {
						"scripted_bar_progress(kmtd_internal_struggle_bar)" >= 400
					}
				}
			}
			triggered_desc = {
				desc = sino_event.70.d.b
				trigger = {
					scope:journal_entry = {
						"scripted_bar_progress(kmtd_internal_struggle_bar)" < 400
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	flavor = sino_event.70.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	cooldown = {
		years = 5
	}
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:journal_entry = {
					"scripted_bar_progress(kmtd_internal_struggle_bar)" >= 480
				}
			}
			remove_variable = black_sun_win_var
			set_variable = wihte_sun_win_var
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:journal_entry = {
					"scripted_bar_progress(kmtd_internal_struggle_bar)" < 480
				}
			}
			remove_variable = white_sun_win_var
			set_variable = black_sun_win_var
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = kmt_meeting_var
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = white_sun_over
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.70.a
		trigger = {
			has_variable = wihte_sun_win_var
			NOT = {
				has_variable = kmt_1_var				#1大
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = new_smzy_modifier
			months = 240
			is_decaying = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = kmt_1_var
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.70.b
		trigger = {
			has_variable = wihte_sun_win_var
			has_variable = kmt_1_var
			NOT = {
				has_variable = kmt_2_var
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = new_smzy_modifier1			#2大
			months = 240
			is_decaying = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = kmt_2_var
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.70.c
		trigger = {
			has_variable = kmt_2_var
			has_variable = wihte_sun_win_var
			has_variable = kmt_1_var
			NOT = {
				has_variable = kmt_3_var
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = new_smzy_modifier3			#3大
			months = 240
			is_decaying = yes
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			set_variable = kmt_3_var

sino_event.71 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/dsk_event_pictures/fangeming.png"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"
	title = sino_event.71.t
	desc = sino_event.71.d
	flavor = sino_event.71.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	cooldown = {
		years = 100
	}
	trigger = {
		scope:journal_entry = {
			"scripted_bar_progress(kmtd_internal_struggle_bar)" < 100
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = china_fascist_var
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = china_fascist_var
		every_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_trotskyist
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_maoist
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_communist
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_vanguardist
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_anarchist
				}
			}
			exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
		}
		every_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_trait = cruel
					has_trait = bandit
					has_trait = grifter
					has_trait = pillager
				}
			}
			set_ideology = ideology:ideology_fascist
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.71.a
		custom_tooltip = fangeming
		default_option = yes
		activate_law = law_type:law_one_party_state
		activate_law = law_type:law_secret_police
		activate_law = law_type:law_IJ_defamation
		add_radicals = {
			value = 0.2
			pop_type = laborers
		}
		add_radicals = {
			value = 0.2
			pop_type = farmers
		}
		ig:ig_petty_bourgeoisie = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = victory_of_conservatism
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ig:ig_armed_forces = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = victory_of_conservatism
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ig:ig_rural_folk = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = ig_mass_arrests
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ig:ig_trade_unions = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = ig_mass_arrests
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		je:je_whitesun_redland = {
			remove_modifier = new_smzy_modifier6
			add_modifier = new_smzy_modifier2
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_fascist
				}
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

	type = country_event

	desc = sino_event.94.d

		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/soe_event_pictures/japan6.dds"
	}

	dlc = dlc_ra_plus

	minor_right_icon = c:JAP
	trigger = {
	}

		c:KOR ?= {
			save_scope_as = kor_scope
		}
		c:CHI ?= {
			je:je_china_and_japan = {
				add_progress = {
					value = 1
					name = japan_china_progress_bar
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.94.a
		create_diplomatic_pact = {
			country = c:KOR
			type = puppet
		}
		c:KOR ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = sick_china
			}
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:RUS
			value = -20
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:GBR
			value = -10
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:USA
			value = -10
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:CHI
			value = -50
		}
		c:CHI ?= {
			create_diplomatic_pact = {
				country = root
				type = rivalry
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.94.b
		highlighted_option = yes
		show_as_unavailable = {
			always = yes
		}
		trigger = {
			c:KOR ?= {
				is_player = no
				is_subject_of = ROOT
			}
		}
		annex_with_incorporation = c:KOR
		change_infamy = 30
		change_relations = {
			country = c:RUS
			value = -80
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:CHI
			value = -80
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:GBR
			value = -20
		}
		change_relations = {
			country = c:USA
			value = -20
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:CHI
				c:CHI ?= {
					is_player = no
				}
			}
			c:CHI ?= {
				create_diplomatic_pact = {
					country = root
					type = rivalry
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:RUS
				c:RUS ?= {
					is_player = no
				}
			}
			c:RUS ?= {
				create_diplomatic_pact = {
					country = root
					type = rivalry
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.94.c
		trigger = {
			exists = c:KOR
			has_claim = s:STATE_SEOUL
		}
		c:JAP ?= {
			create_diplomatic_play = {
				war = no
				type = dp_return_state
				target_state = s:STATE_SEOUL.region_state:KOR
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:JAP
					type = return_state
					target_state = s:STATE_BUSAN.region_state:KOR
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:JAP
					type = return_state
					target_state = s:STATE_PYONGYANG.region_state:KOR
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:JAP
					type = return_state
					target_state = s:STATE_SARIWON.region_state:KOR
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:JAP
					type = return_state
					target_state = s:STATE_YANGHO.region_state:KOR
				}
			}
		}

		}

sino_event.103 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = sino_event.103.t
	desc = sino_event.103.d
	flavor = sino_event.103.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/soe_event_pictures/japan4.dds"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/military_parade"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/jap1.dds"
	trigger = {
	}
	immediate = {
		set_global_variable = dsk_saigo_takamori_happen
		create_country = {
			tag = SMF
			origin = c:JAP
			state = s:STATE_KYUSHU.region_state:JAP
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = agitator_saigo_takamori
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = agitator_saigo_takamori
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = agitator_saigo_takamori
					}
					exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
					transfer_character = c:SMF
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
					set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
					add_character_role = general
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = agitator_saigo_takamori
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = agitator_saigo_takamori
				}
				transfer_character = c:SMF
				set_character_as_ruler = yes
				set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
				add_character_role = general
			}
		}
		else = {
			c:SMF = {
				create_character = {
					template = agitator_saigo_takamori
					on_created = {
						set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
						set_character_as_ruler = yes
						add_character_role = general
					}
				}
			}
		}
		c:SMF ?= {
			save_scope_as = smf_scope
			ruler ?= {
				save_scope_as = ruler_scope
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.103.a
		default_option = yes
		add_radicals = {
			value = 0.1
			culture = cu:japanese
			pop_type = soldiers
		}
		c:SMF ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_taiping_hiring_bonus
				years = 1
			}
		}
		create_diplomatic_play = {
			target_country = c:SMF
			war = yes
			type = dp_annex_war
			annex_as_civil_war = yes
		}
		change_infamy = -50
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_annex_smf
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				is_player = yes
			}
			post_notification = xinan_war
		}
	}
}

sino_event.112 = {
	#王政复古
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/soe_event_pictures/japan3.dds"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/military_parade"
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/japan.dds"
	title = sino_event.112.t
	desc = sino_event.112.d
	flavor = sino_event.112.f
	immediate = {
		set_strategy = ai_strategy_meiji_restoration_2
		c:USA ?= {
			save_scope_as = usa_scope
		}
		c:GBR ?= {
			save_scope_as = gbr_scope
		}
		c:NET ?= {
			save_scope_as = net_scope
		}
		c:RUS ?= {
			save_scope_as = rus_scope
		}
		c:FRA ?= {
			save_scope_as = fra_scope
		}
		ig:ig_landowners = {
			save_scope_as = shougun_ig
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = shougun_ig_general
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.112.a
		change_variable = {
			name = shogunate_var
			add = 120
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_law = law_type:law_bakufu
			}
			activate_law = law_type:law_oligarchy
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					has_variable = japan_emperor_restored
				}
			}
			#	trigger_event = {
			#		id = meiji.1
			#	}
			set_variable = japan_emperor_restored
		}
		set_variable = japan_restoration_complete
		set_global_variable = dsk_saigo_takamori_happen
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = agitator_saigo_takamori
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = agitator_saigo_takamori
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = agitator_saigo_takamori
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
					add_character_role = general
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = agitator_saigo_takamori
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = agitator_saigo_takamori
				}
				transfer_character = ROOT
				set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
				add_character_role = general
			}
		}
		else = {
			create_character = {
				template = agitator_saigo_takamori
				ig_leader = yes
				is_general = yes
				on_created = {
					set_career_length = {
						years = 50
						random_range = {
							1
							1.5
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = finish_meiji_restoration_tt
		hidden_effect = {
			trigger_event = {
				id = open_japan.9
				days = 45
				popup = yes
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_player = yes
				}
			}
			post_notification = dazheng_fenghuan
		}
	}
}

# 保守派政变
sino_event.121 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	title = sino_event.121.t
	desc = sino_event.121.d
	flavor = sino_event.121.f
	event_image = {
		video = votp_cops_march
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_scales.dds"
	cooldown = {
		years = 100
	}
	trigger = {
		scope:journal_entry = {
			scripted_bar_progress = {
				name = je_difficult_china_progress_bar
				value < 50
			}
		}
		ig:ig_landowners = {
			NOT = {
				has_modifier = victory_of_conservatism
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		ig:ig_landowners = {
			save_scope_as = landowners_ig
			leader = {
				save_scope_as = landowners_ig_general
			}
		}
		every_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				is_in_government = yes
			}
			remove_ruling_interest_group = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.121.a
		default_option = yes
		activate_law = law_type:law_censorship
		activate_law = law_type:law_autocracy
		activate_law = law_type:law_freedom_of_conscience
		#activate_law = law_type:law_national_supremacy
		if = {
			limit = {
				ig:ig_landowners = {
					is_in_government = no
				}
			}
			ig:ig_landowners = {
				add_ruling_interest_group = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				ig:ig_armed_forces = {
					is_in_government = no
				}
			}
			ig:ig_armed_forces = {
				add_ruling_interest_group = yes
			}
		}
		ig:ig_landowners = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = victory_of_conservatism
				months = 24
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_autocracy_of_the_tsar
			months = 120
			is_decaying = yes
		}
		ig:ig_intelligentsia = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = ig_mass_arrests
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = ig_mass_arrests
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = sino_event.121.tt
			set_variable = {
				name = Pentecostal_Variation_fail_var
				years = 1
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.81.c
		trigger = {
			has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_secret_police
		}
		scope:landowners_ig_general = {
			interest_group = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = dangerous_be_killed
					days = normal_modifier_time
					is_decaying = yes
				}
			}
			kill_character = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 99
		}
	}
}

sino_event.141 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/soe_event_pictures/guangxu.png"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/europenorthamerica/before_the_battle"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/Hail_to_the_Qing.png"
	title = sino_event.141.t
	desc = sino_event.141.d
	flavor = sino_event.141.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	cooldown = {
		years = 100
	}
	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = chi_cixi_template
			}
			save_scope_as = ecchi_chi_cixi_character_benefactor
		}
		scope:ecchi_chi_cixi_character_benefactor ?= {
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = china_reform_var
			years = 1
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.141.a
		default_option = yes
		#activate_law = law_type:law_guaranteed_liberties
		ruler ?= {
			add_trait = ambitious
			remove_trait = political_appointee
		}
		scope:ecchi_chi_cixi_character_benefactor = {
			interest_group = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = dangerous_be_killed
					days = normal_modifier_time
					is_decaying = yes
				}
			}
			kill_character = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.141.b
		ruler ?= {
			add_trait = ambitious
			remove_trait = political_appointee
		}
		scope:ecchi_chi_cixi_character_benefactor = {
			interest_group = {
				add_modifier = {
					name = dangerous_be_killed
					days = normal_modifier_time
					is_decaying = yes
				}
			}
			add_trait = wounded
			add_trait = scarred
			add_trait = deathly_ill
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
}

	type = country_event

	}

	}

		set_variable = wuxu_bianfa_die
		ig:ig_landowners = {
			save_scope_as = landowners_ig

				save_scope_as = landowners_ig_general
			}
		}
		every_interest_group = {
			limit = {
				is_in_government = yes
			}
			remove_ruling_interest_group = yes
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = ecchi_chi_liang_qichao_character_template
			}
			save_scope_as = ecchi_chi_liang_qichao_character_benefactor
		}
		scope:landowners_ig_general ?= {
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						is_immortal = yes
					}
					set_character_immortal = no
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.121.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:ecchi_chi_liang_qichao_character_benefactor = {
			exile_character_with_martyrdom = yes
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = china_fail_tt			#增加救亡图存的进度条
			je:je_china_19th_century = {
				add_progress = {
					value = 1
					name = china_reformist_progress_bar
				}
			}
		}
		activate_law = law_type:law_censorship
		activate_law = law_type:law_autocracy
		activate_law = law_type:law_freedom_of_conscience
		#activate_law = law_type:law_national_supremacy
		if = {
			limit = {
				ig:ig_landowners = {
					is_in_government = no
				}
			}
			ig:ig_landowners = {
				add_ruling_interest_group = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				ig:ig_armed_forces = {
					is_in_government = no
				}
			}
			ig:ig_armed_forces = {
				add_ruling_interest_group = yes
			}
		}
		ig:ig_landowners = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = victory_of_conservatism
				months = 24
			}
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_autocracy_of_the_tsar
			months = 120
			is_decaying = yes
		}
		ig:ig_intelligentsia = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = ig_mass_arrests
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		ig:ig_industrialists = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = ig_mass_arrests
				days = normal_modifier_time
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = sino_event.121.tt
			set_variable = {
				name = IF_Pentecostal_Variation_fail_var
				years = 1
			}
		}
		option = {
			name = clever_dsk.81.c
			trigger = {
				has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_secret_police
			}
			scope:landowners_ig_general = {
				interest_group = {

sino_event.153 = {
	#德意日三国轴心彩蛋？

	title = sino_event.153.t
	desc = sino_event.153.d
	flavor = sino_event.153.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	trigger = {
		NOT = {
			has_variable = sino_event.153_var
		}
		exists = c:JAP
		exists = c:ITA
		exists = c:GER
		c:GER ?= this
		game_date > 1931.2.1		#19世纪蹦出个德意日协定很难绷
		c:ITA ?= {
			is_subject = no
			relations:root >= relations_threshold:amicable
		}
		c:JAP ?= {
			is_subject = no
			country_rank >= rank_value:major_power
			relations:root >= relations_threshold:amicable
		}
	}

		c:JAP ?= {
			save_scope_as = jap_scope

			}

			}
		}

			ruler ?= {
				save_scope_as = crisis_scope_2
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.153.a
		hidden_effect = {
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					is_player = yes
					NOT = {
						c:GER ?= this
					}
				}
				trigger_event = {
					id = sino_event.159

						3
						5
					}
				}
			}
			c:GER ?= {
				set_secret_goal = {
					country = c:ITA
					secret_goal = befriend
				}
				set_secret_goal = {
					country = c:JAP
					secret_goal = befriend
				}
			}
			c:ITA ?= {
				set_secret_goal = {
					country = c:GER
					secret_goal = befriend
				}
				set_secret_goal = {
					country = c:JAP
					secret_goal = befriend
				}
			}
			c:JAP ?= {
				set_secret_goal = {
					country = c:ITA
					secret_goal = befriend
				}
				set_secret_goal = {
					country = c:GER
					secret_goal = befriend
				}
			}
		}
	}

		}
		s:STATE_KIRGHIZIA = {
			add_claim = c:RUS
		}
		s:STATE_ALTAI = {
			add_claim = c:RUS
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.180.b
		trigger = {
			has_variable = china_difficult_var
		}
		custom_tooltip = china_is_so_difficult_tt
		s:STATE_JETISY = {
			add_claim = c:RUS
		}
		s:STATE_KIRGHIZIA = {
			add_claim = c:RUS
		}
		s:STATE_ALTAI = {
			add_claim = c:RUS
		}
		c:RUS ?= {
			create_diplomatic_play = {
				name = great_game_war
				type = dp_return_state
				target_country = c:CHI
				target_state = s:STATE_JETISY.region_state:CHI
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:RUS
					type = return_state
					target_state = s:STATE_ALTAI.region_state:CHI
				}
			}
			change_relations = {
				country = c:CHI
				value = -50
			}
			set_secret_goal = {
				country = c:CHI
				secret_goal = conquer
			}
			every_diplomatic_play = {
				limit = {
					is_war = yes
					is_diplomatic_play_participant = c:XIN
					NOT = {
						is_diplomatic_play_participant = c:XIN
					}
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						initiator_is = c:XIN
					}
					add_initiator_backers = {
						c:RUS
					}
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						target_is = c:XIN
					}
					add_target_backers = {
						c:RUS
					}
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

sino_event.181 = {
	#九一八事变
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	event_image = {
		video = "japempire"
	}
	gui_window = event_window_widescreen_classic

	desc = sino_event.165.d
	flavor = sino_event.165.f

	minor_right_icon = c:JAP
	cooldown = {
		years = 20
	}
	trigger = {
		c:JAP ?= {
			is_ai = yes
		}
		OR = {
			#考虑到日志完成时，很有可能还在一战，会影响AI状态

		}
	}

		cu:chinese_culture = {
			save_scope_as = chinese_culture
		}

			}
		}

			save_scope_as = usa_scope
		}

			add_journal_entry = {
				type = je_manchuliya
			}
			save_scope_as = jap_scope
		}
		c:GBR ?= {
			save_scope_as = gbr_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.165.a
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = china_fail_tt			#增加救亡图存的进度条
			je:je_china_19th_century = {
				add_progress = {
					value = 1
					name = china_reformist_progress_bar
				}
			}
		}
		c:JAP ?= {
			change_infamy = -60
			create_diplomatic_play = {
				name = invade_china_war
				target_state = s:STATE_SHENGJING.region_state:CHI
				escalation = 75
				type = dp_return_state
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:JAP
					type = return_state
					target_state = s:STATE_SOUTHERN_MANCHURIA.region_state:CHI
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:JAP
					type = return_state
					target_state = s:STATE_NORTHERN_MANCHURIA.region_state:CHI
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:JAP
					type = conquer_state
					target_state = s:STATE_AMUR.region_state:CHI
				}
				add_war_goal = {
					holder = c:JAP
					type = conquer_state
					target_state = s:STATE_OUTER_MANCHURIA.region_state:CHI
				}
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
}

sino_event.185 = {
	#汉人起义
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:han_state
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/soe_event_pictures/Beijing_Castle_Boxer_Rebellion_1900_FINAL.jpg"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/Hail_to_the_Qing.png"
	title = sino_event.185.t
	desc = sino_event.185.d
	flavor = sino_event.185.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	cooldown = {
		years = 3
	}
	minor_left_icon = c:CHI
	minor_right_icon = root
	trigger = {
		exists = c:CHI
		NOT = {
			has_discrimination_trait = tradition_sinosphere
		}
		AND = {
			has_technology_researched = nationalism
			c:CHI ?= {
				has_technology_researched = nationalism
			}
		}
		is_subject = no
		any_scope_state = {
			lca_is_chinese_state = yes
			is_treaty_port = no
			is_split_state = no			#不是分属地区，因为条约港失效会变
			NOT = {
				has_modifier = modifier_han_geming
				has_modifier = modifier_han_kill
			}
			OR = {
				state_region = s:STATE_SOUTHERN_MANCHURIA
				state_region = s:STATE_NORTHERN_MANCHURIA
				state_region = {
					is_homeland = cu:han
				}
			}
			radical_fraction = {
				value >= 0.25
				culture = cu:han
			}
		}
		AND = {
			country_average_cultural_acceptance = {
				target = cu:han
				value < acceptance_status_5
			}
			cultural_acceptance_base = {
				target = cu:han
				value < acceptance_status_5
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		ruler ?= {
			save_scope_as = empire_character
		}
		ROOT = {
			save_scope_as = scope_yolo
		}
		cu:chinese_culture = {
			save_scope_as = chinese_culture
		}
		cu:han = {
			save_scope_as = han_culture
		}
		c:CHI ?= {
			save_scope_as = chi_scope
			ruler ?= {
				save_scope_as = crisis_scope_2
			}
		}
		c:RUS ?= {
			save_scope_as = rus_scope
		}
		c:JAP ?= {
			save_scope_as = jap_scope
		}
		c:GBR ?= {
			save_scope_as = gbr_scope
		}
		random_scope_state = {
			limit = {
				lca_is_chinese_state = yes
				is_treaty_port = no
				is_split_state = no
				OR = {
					state_region = s:STATE_SOUTHERN_MANCHURIA
					state_region = s:STATE_NORTHERN_MANCHURIA
					state_region = {
						is_homeland = cu:han
					}
				}
				radical_fraction = {
					value >= 0.25
					culture = cu:han
				}
			}
			save_scope_as = han_state
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.185.a
		default_option = yes
		add_treasury = add_money_small_chi_gdp
		c:CHI ?= {
			add_treasury = spend_money_small_chi_gdp
		}
		change_infamy = -15
		scope:han_state = {
			set_state_owner = c:CHI
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_player = yes
				}
			}
			post_notification = return_han_state
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					c:JAP ?= this
				}
				add = -100
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.185.b
		scope:han_state = {
			add_radicals = {
				value = 0.1
				culture = cu:han
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_han_geming
				years = 5
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					c:JAP ?= this
				}
				add = 100
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.185.c
		change_infamy = 10
		scope:han_state = {
			state_region = {
				add_devastation = 30
			}
			kill_population_percent_in_state = {
				percent = 0.02
				culture = cu:han
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = modifier_han_kill
				years = 10
			}
		}
		c:CHI ?= {
			change_relations = {
				country = root
				value = -100
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					c:JAP ?= this
				}
				scope:empire_character = {
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = a_war_criminal
						has_modifier = b_war_criminal
						has_modifier = c_war_criminal
					}
				}
			}
			scope:empire_character = {
				add_modifier = c_war_criminal
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					c:JAP ?= this
				}
				scope:empire_character = {
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = a_war_criminal
						has_modifier = b_war_criminal
					}
					has_modifier = c_war_criminal
				}
			}
			scope:empire_character = {
				remove_modifier = c_war_criminal
				add_modifier = b_war_criminal
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					c:JAP ?= this
				}
				scope:empire_character = {
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = a_war_criminal
						has_modifier = c_war_criminal
					}
					has_modifier = b_war_criminal
				}
			}
			scope:empire_character = {
				remove_modifier = b_war_criminal
				add_modifier = a_war_criminal
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = diplomatic_tragedy_tt
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_north_china
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_south_china
					}
					NOT = {
						c:CHI ?= this
					}
					NOT = {
						this = root
					}
				}
				change_relations = {
					country = root
					value = -30
				}
				create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
					type = catalyst_event_negative
					target = root
				}
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_player = yes
				}
			}
			post_notification = slaughter_human
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					c:JAP ?= this
				}
				add = 100
			}
		}
	}
}

sino_event.195 = {
	#袁世凯成为军队领袖
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = clever_dsk.39.t
	desc = clever_dsk.39.d
	flavor = clever_dsk.39.f
	duration = 1
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:university_benefactor
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_newspaper.dds"
	trigger = {
		country_has_primary_culture = cu:han
		game_date > 1900.1.1
		game_date < 1916.6.6
		ig:ig_armed_forces = {
			ig_counts_as_marginal = no
		}
		NOT = {
			any_scope_character = {
				has_template = chi_general_yuan_shikai
				ig_leader = yes
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = shikai_become_leader
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				any_country = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = chi_general_yuan_shikai
					}
				}
			}
			random_country = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = chi_general_yuan_shikai
					}
				}
				random_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = chi_general_yuan_shikai
					}
					transfer_character = ROOT
					set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_character_in_exile_pool = {
					has_template = chi_general_yuan_shikai
				}
			}
			random_character_in_exile_pool = {
				limit = {
					has_template = chi_general_yuan_shikai
				}
				transfer_character = ROOT
				set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			create_character = {
				template = chi_general_yuan_shikai
				ig_leader = yes
			}
			set_global_variable = ecchi_shikai_happen
		}
		set_variable = shikai_become_leader
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = chi_general_yuan_shikai
			}
			save_scope_as = university_benefactor
		}
	}
	option = {
		default_option = yes
		name = clever_dsk.39.a
		trigger = {
		}
		scope:university_benefactor = {
			add_character_role = politician
			set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 99
		}
	}
}

sino_event.201 = {
	#蒙古独立
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = sino_event.201.t
	desc = sino_event.201.d
	flavor = sino_event.201.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	event_image = {
		video = "asia_union_leader"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/china.dds"
	cooldown = {
		years = 100
	}
	trigger = {
		scope:journal_entry = {
			"scripted_bar_progress(five_races_harmony_bar)" <= 120
		}
		NOT = {
			exists = c:MGL
		}
		OR = {
			owns_entire_state_region = STATE_ULIASTAI
			owns_entire_state_region = STATE_URGA
		}
		any_scope_state = {
			OR = {
				state_region = s:STATE_ULIASTAI
				state_region = s:STATE_URGA
			}
			radical_fraction = {
				value > 0.33
				culture = cu:mongol
			}
		}
		OR = {
			is_player = yes
			AND = {
				is_player = no
				game_date > 1911.2.1
				game_date < 1916.1.1
			}
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = sino_event_mongol_depend
			has_variable = sino_event_130_var_a
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = sino_event_mongol_depend
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:CHI
				NOT = {
					exists = c:MGL
				}
			}
			create_country = {
				tag = MGL
				origin = c:CHI
				state = s:STATE_ULIASTAI.region_state:CHI
				state = s:STATE_URGA.region_state:CHI
			}
			c:MGL ?= {
				activate_law = law_type:law_theocracy
				every_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = ecchi_mgl_bogd_khan_character_template
					}
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
					set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:ROC
				NOT = {
					exists = c:CHI
				}
				NOT = {
					exists = c:MGL
				}
			}
			create_country = {
				tag = MGL
				origin = c:ROC
				state = s:STATE_ULIASTAI.region_state:ROC
				state = s:STATE_URGA.region_state:ROC
			}
			c:MGL ?= {
				activate_law = law_type:law_theocracy
				every_scope_character = {
					limit = {
						has_template = ecchi_mgl_bogd_khan_character_template
					}
					set_character_as_ruler = yes
					set_as_interest_group_leader = yes
				}
			}
		}
		s:STATE_ULIASTAI = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:MGL
					owner ?= {
						OR = {
							c:CHI ?= this
							is_subject_of = c:CHI
							c:ROC ?= this
							is_subject_of = c:ROC
						}
					}
				}
				set_state_owner = c:MGL
			}
		}
		s:STATE_URGA = {
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:MGL
					owner ?= {
						OR = {
							c:CHI ?= this
							is_subject_of = c:CHI
							c:ROC ?= this
							is_subject_of = c:ROC
						}
					}
				}
				set_state_owner = c:MGL
			}
		}
		c:MGL ?= {
			save_scope_as = mgl_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.201.a
		default_option = yes
		add_radicals = {
			value = 0.22
			culture = cu:mongol
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.201.b
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:MGL
				exists = c:RUS
				c:RUS ?= {
					is_player = no
					country_rank >= rank_value:great_power
				}
				c:MGL ?= {
					is_player = no
					is_subject = no
				}
			}
			c:RUS ?= {
				create_diplomatic_pact = {
					country = c:MGL
					type = puppet
				}
			}
		}
		create_diplomatic_play = {
			name = huguo_war
			escalation = 60
			type = dp_annex_war
			target_country = c:MGL
			target_state = s:STATE_URGA.region_state:MGL
		}
		change_infamy = -50
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
}

sino_event.203 = {
	#隐藏事件，蒙古变俄罗斯受保护国
	type = country_event
	hidden = yes
	trigger = {
		c:CHI ?= this
		c:RUS ?= {
			is_ai = yes
			country_rank >= rank_value:great_power
			has_secret_goal = {
				who = root
				secret_goal = conquer
			}
		}

	title = sino_event.217.t
	desc = sino_event.217.d
	flavor = sino_event.217.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	trigger = {
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		scope:journal_entry = {
			"scripted_bar_progress(china_reformist_progress_bar)" >= 3
		}
		year >= 1868
		NOT = {
			has_variable = luhaodong_var
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = luhaodong_var
		c:CHI ?= {
			save_scope_as = chi_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.217.a
		default_option = yes
		s:STATE_NANJING = {
			start_harvest_condition = optimal_sunlight
		}
		add_modifier = {
			name = white_sun_modifier
			years = 5
		}
	}
}

sino_event.220 = {
	#征服日本前
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = sino_event.220.t
	desc = sino_event.220.d
	flavor = sino_event.220.f
	gui_window = event_window_superevent_modern
	event_image = {
		video = "rocjap"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/japan.dds"
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	trigger = {
	}
	minor_left_icon = c:JAP
	minor_right_icon = root
	immediate = {
		ruler ?= {
			save_scope_as = ruler
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_role_of_type = general
				commander_rank <= 4
				is_ruler_of_own_country = no
			}
			save_scope_as = army_soldier
			interest_group = {
				save_scope_as = low_rank_general_ig
			}
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_role_of_type = admiral
				commander_rank <= 4
				is_ruler_of_own_country = no
			}
			save_scope_as = navy_soldier
			interest_group = {
				save_scope_as = a_low_rank_general_ig
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.220.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:army_soldier = {
			add_commander_rank = 1
			add_modifier = china_army
			add_trait = charismatic
		}
		scope:low_rank_general_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = low_rank_general_ig_opinion_add
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.220.b
		default_option = yes
		scope:navy_soldier = {
			add_commander_rank = 1
			add_modifier = china_navy
			add_trait = charismatic
		}
		scope:a_low_rank_general_ig = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = low_rank_general_ig_opinion_add
				days = short_modifier_time
			}
		}
	}
}

	title = sino_event.221.t
	desc = sino_event.221.d
	flavor = sino_event.221.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	trigger = {
	}
	immediate = {
		c:JAP ?= {
			save_scope_as = jap_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.221.a
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = modifier_newroc
			months = 120
		}
		cu:japanese = {
			remove_tradition_trait = coprosperity_circle
			add_tradition_trait = tradition_sinosphere
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				exists = c:JAP
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = japan_will_change_name_tt
				c:JAP ?= {
					add_primary_culture = cu:han
					set_variable = wonu_var
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sino_event.222 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:japs_state
	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_fire"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/military_parade"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/chiempire.png"
	title = sino_event.222.t
	desc = sino_event.222.d
	flavor = sino_event.222.f
	cooldown = {
		years = 1
	}
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	trigger = {
		has_war_with = c:JAP
		c:JAP ?= {
			any_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					devastation > 0.1
					is_incorporated = yes
				}
			}
		}
		has_modifier = kill_japs_modifier
		any_interest_group = {
			OR = {
				leader = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_chinaempire
				}
				leader = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_fascist
				}
			}
			is_in_government = yes
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_ideology = ideology:ideology_chinaempire
				is_in_government = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = empire_character
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_ideology = ideology:ideology_fascist
				is_in_government = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = fascist_character
		}
		c:JAP ?= {
			save_scope_as = jap_scope
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					devastation > 0.1
					is_incorporated = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = japs_state
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.222.a
		custom_tooltip = no_fascist_character
		change_infamy = 10
		scope:japs_state = {
			kill_population_percent_in_state = {
				percent = 0.1
				culture = cu:japanese
			}
		}
	}
}

sino_event.225 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = sino_event.225.t
	desc = sino_event.225.d
	flavor = sino_event.225.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:crisis_scope_2
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/asia_union_leader"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"
	cooldown = {
		years = 100
	}
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:CHI
				root = c:CHI
			}
			AND = {
				exists = c:ROC
				c:ROC = ROOT
			}
		}
		country_has_primary_culture = cu:manchu
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		NOT = {
			has_variable = is_empiredie_1
		}
		game_date > 1861.1.1
		any_scope_character = {
			has_template = chi_xianfeng_template
		}
		game_date < 1862.1.1
	}
	immediate = {
		ruler ?= {
			set_variable = {
				name = ecchi_27_shengbi
			}
			save_scope_as = crisis_scope_2
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = is_empiredie_1
			value = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.225.a
		default_option = yes
		s:STATE_BEIJING = {
			start_harvest_condition = moderate_rainfall
		}
		every_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = chi_xianfeng_template
			}
			kill_character = yes
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.225.b
	}
}

sino_event.228 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = sino_event.228.t
	desc = sino_event.228.d
	flavor = sino_event.228.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:crisis_scope_2
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/asia_union_leader"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/china.dds"
	cooldown = {
		years = 100
	}
	trigger = {
		is_player = yes
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:CHI
				root = c:CHI
			}
			AND = {
				exists = c:ROC
				c:ROC = ROOT
			}
		}
		country_has_primary_culture = cu:manchu
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		NOT = {
			has_variable = is_empire_1
		}
		NOT = {
			any_scope_character = {
				has_template = 27_chi_zaichun_template
			}
		}
		game_date < 1875.1.12
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_chi_empire1_king
			NOT = {
				#不能取代自己的父亲
				heir ?= {
					has_template = chi_xianfeng_template
				}
			}
		}
		game_date > 1858.4.27
	}
	immediate = {
		ruler ?= {
			save_scope_as = crisis_scope_2
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = is_empire_1
			value = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.228.a
		default_option = yes
		s:STATE_BEIJING = {
			start_harvest_condition = optimal_sunlight
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = chi_daoguang_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = chi_xianfeng_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_chi_zaichun_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = chi_daoguang_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = chi_xianfeng_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_chi_zaichun_template
				ruler = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.228.b
	}
}

		name = sino_event.229.a

		s:STATE_BEIJING = {
			start_harvest_condition = optimal_sunlight
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = chi_daoguang_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = chi_xianfeng_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_chi_zaichun_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_chi_zaitian_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = chi_daoguang_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = chi_xianfeng_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_chi_zaichun_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_chi_zaitian_template
				ruler = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.229.b
	}
}

sino_event.230 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = sino_event.230.t
	desc = sino_event.230.d
	flavor = sino_event.230.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:crisis_scope_2
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/asia_union_leader"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/china.dds"
	cooldown = {
		years = 100
	}
	trigger = {
		is_player = yes
		OR = {
			AND = {
				exists = c:CHI
				root = c:CHI
			}
			AND = {
				exists = c:ROC
				c:ROC = ROOT
			}
		}
		country_has_primary_culture = cu:manchu
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		NOT = {
			has_variable = is_empire_3
		}
		NOT = {
			any_scope_character = {
				has_template = 27_chi_puyi_template
			}
		}
		game_date < 1967.10.17
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = 27_chi_empire3_king
			NOT = {
				heir ?= {
					has_template = 27_chi_zaichun_template
				}
				heir ?= {
					has_template = 27_chi_zaitian_template
				}
			}
		}
		game_date > 1908.2.7
	}
	immediate = {
		ruler ?= {
			save_scope_as = crisis_scope_2
		}
		set_variable = {
			name = is_empire_3
			value = yes
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.230.a
		default_option = yes
		s:STATE_BEIJING = {
			start_harvest_condition = optimal_sunlight
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = chi_daoguang_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = chi_xianfeng_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_chi_zaichun_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_chi_zaitian_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_chi_puyi_template
				heir = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = chi_daoguang_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = chi_xianfeng_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_chi_zaichun_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
					any_scope_character = {
						has_template = 27_chi_zaitian_template
						is_ruler_of_own_country = yes
					}
				}
			}
			create_character = {
				template = 27_chi_puyi_template
				ruler = yes
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.230.b
	}

#他日波涛如山
sino_event.231 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = sino_event.231.t
	desc = sino_event.231.d
	flavor = sino_event.231.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_superevent_modern
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/dsk_event_pictures/love_can_conquer_sea.png"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/invention_icons/romanticism.dds"
	minor_left_icon = c:CHI
	minor_right_icon = c:JAP
	cooldown = {
		years = 10
	}
	trigger = {
		is_player = yes
		c:CHI ?= {
			any_scope_war = {
				AND = {
					is_war_participant = c:JAP
					has_war_support = {
						target = c:JAP
						value >= 50
					}
					has_war_support = {
						target = c:CHI
						value <= 20
					}
				}
				war_has_active_peace_deal = no
			}
		}
		c:JAP ?= {
			has_war_with = c:CHI
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		c:CHI ?= {
			random_scope_war = {
				save_scope_as = ongoing_acw
			}
		}
		cu:japanese = {
			save_scope_as = japanese
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.231.a
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					c:CHI ?= this
					c:ROC ?= this
				}
			}
			add_culture_acceptance_modifier = {
				#对日本文化接受度低到冰点
				culture = scope:japanese
				multiplier = -50
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = add_protect_china_war_bar_tt
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					OR = {
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_north_china
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_south_china
					}
					NOT = {
						c:CHI ?= this
					}
					NOT = {
						c:JAP ?= this
					}
				}
				create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
					type = catalyst_event_positive
					target = c:CHI
				}
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					has_journal_entry = je_protect_china
				}
				je:je_protect_china = {
					add_progress = {
						value = 1
						name = je_protect_china_war_bar
					}
				}
			}
		}
		scope:ongoing_acw = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:ROC
				}
				add_war_war_support = {
					target = c:ROC
					value = 100
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:CHI
				}
				add_war_war_support = {
					target = c:CHI
					value = 100
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

sino_event.232 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = sino_event.232.t
	desc = sino_event.232.d
	flavor = sino_event.232.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_superevent_modern

		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/dsk_event_pictures/japs_will_die.png"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/invention_icons/romanticism.dds"
	minor_left_icon = c:CHI
	minor_right_icon = c:JAP
	cooldown = {
		years = 10
	}
	trigger = {
		is_player = yes
		c:CHI ?= {
			any_scope_war = {
				AND = {
					is_war_participant = c:JAP
					has_war_support = {
						target = c:JAP
						value >= 50
					}
					has_war_support = {
						target = c:CHI
						value <= 30
					}
				}
				war_has_active_peace_deal = no
			}
		}
		c:JAP ?= {
			has_war_with = c:CHI
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		c:CHI ?= {
			random_scope_war = {
				save_scope_as = ongoing_acw
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.232.a
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = add_protect_china_war_bar_tt
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					AND = {
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_north_china
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_south_china
					}
					NOT = {
						c:CHI ?= this
					}
					NOT = {
						c:JAP ?= this
					}
				}
				create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
					type = catalyst_event_positive
					target = c:CHI
				}
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					has_journal_entry = je_protect_china
				}
				je:je_protect_china = {
					add_progress = {
						value = 1
						name = je_protect_china_war_bar
					}
				}
			}
		}
		c:CHI ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = protect_homeland_modifier						#
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = protect_homeland_modifier
					years = 20
				}
			}
		}
		scope:ongoing_acw = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:ROC
				}
				add_war_war_support = {
					target = c:ROC
					value = 50
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:CHI
				}
				add_war_war_support = {
					target = c:CHI
					value = 50
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

#死字旗
sino_event.233 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = sino_event.233.t
	desc = sino_event.233.d
	flavor = sino_event.233.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_superevent_modern
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/dsk_event_pictures/save_my_nation.png"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/invention_icons/romanticism.dds"
	minor_left_icon = c:CHI
	minor_right_icon = c:JAP
	cooldown = {
		years = 10
	}
	trigger = {
		is_player = yes
		c:CHI ?= {
			any_scope_war = {
				AND = {
					is_war_participant = c:JAP
					has_war_support = {
						target = c:JAP
						value >= 10
					}
					has_war_support = {
						target = c:CHI
						value <= 10
					}
				}
				war_has_active_peace_deal = no
			}
		}
		c:JAP ?= {
			has_war_with = c:CHI
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		c:CHI ?= {
			random_scope_war = {
				save_scope_as = ongoing_acw
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.233.a
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = add_protect_china_war_bar_tt
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					has_journal_entry = je_protect_china
				}
				je:je_protect_china = {
					add_progress = {
						value = 1
						name = je_protect_china_war_bar
					}
				}
			}
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					OR = {
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_north_china
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_south_china
					}
					NOT = {
						c:CHI ?= this
					}
					NOT = {
						c:JAP ?= this
					}
				}
				create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
					type = catalyst_event_positive
					target = c:CHI
				}
			}
		}
		c:CHI ?= {
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = save_my_nation_modifier						#protect_homeland_modifier
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = save_my_nation_modifier
					years = 5
				}
			}
		}
		scope:ongoing_acw = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:ROC
				}
				add_war_war_support = {
					target = c:ROC
					value = 50
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:CHI
				}
				add_war_war_support = {
					target = c:CHI
					value = 50
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

#日本投降
sino_event.234 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = sino_event.234.t
	desc = sino_event.234.d
	flavor = sino_event.234.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_widescreen_newspaper
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/dsk_event_pictures/jap_surrender.png"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/roc1.png"
	minor_left_icon = c:CHI
	minor_right_icon = c:USA
	trigger = {
		is_player = yes
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 235_var
		}
	}
	cooldown = {
		years = 1000
	}
	immediate = {
		every_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				initiator_is = c:JAP
				target_is = c:CHI
			}
			end_play = yes
		}
		every_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				initiator_is = c:JAP
				target_is = c:USA
			}
			end_play = yes
		}
		every_diplomatic_play = {
			limit = {
				initiator_is = c:JAP
				target_is = c:GBR
			}
			end_play = yes
		}
		set_variable = 235_var
		c:JAP ?= {
			remove_variable = great_japan_var
			save_scope_as = jap_scope
			ruler ?= {
				save_scope_as = ruler_scope
			}
			every_scope_state = {
				make_independent = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = war_loser_modifier
					}
				}
				add_modifier = {
					name = war_loser_modifier
					years = 25
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.234.a
		c:CHI ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = legendary_modifier
				years = 20
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					c:CHI ?= this
					has_variable = alliance_with_chi_var
				}
			}
			trigger_event = {
				id = sino_event.241
				days = 30
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = alliance_with_chi_var
			}
			add_loyalists = {
				value = 0.1
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = legendary_modifier
				years = 20
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = alliance_with_jap_var
			}
			add_radicals = {
				value = 0.1
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = no_legendary_modifier
				years = 20
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = historical_we_has_won_tt
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						exists = c:KOR
					}
					c:JAP ?= {
						has_state_in_state_region = STATE_SEOUL
					}
				}
				s:STATE_SEOUL = {
					random_scope_state = {
						save_scope_as = otlhanguochengli_scope
					}
				}
			}
			create_country = {
				#创建国家
				tag = KOR
				origin = this
				state = scope:otlhanguochengli_scope
				on_created = {
					#创建时
					activate_law = law_type:law_presidential_republic					#总统共和制
				}
			}
			s:STATE_SEOUL = {
				#首尔
				random_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						exists = c:JAP
						owner ?= {
							OR = {
								c:JAP ?= this
								is_subject_of = c:JAP
							}
						}
					}
					set_state_owner = c:KOR
				}
			}
			s:STATE_YANGHO = {
				#两湖
				random_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						exists = c:JAP
						owner ?= {
							OR = {
								c:JAP ?= this
								is_subject_of = c:JAP
							}
						}
					}
					set_state_owner = c:KOR
				}
			}
			s:STATE_BUSAN = {
				#岭南
				random_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						exists = c:JAP
						owner ?= {
							OR = {
								c:JAP ?= this
								is_subject_of = c:JAP
							}
						}
					}
					set_state_owner = c:KOR
				}
			}
			#接下来是朝鲜
			if = {
				limit = {
					c:JAP ?= {
						has_state_in_state_region = STATE_PYONGYANG
					}
				}
				s:STATE_PYONGYANG = {
					random_scope_state = {
						save_scope_as = otlchaoxianchengli_scope
					}
				}
			}
			create_country = {
				#创建国家
				tag = KOR
				origin = this
				state = scope:otlchaoxianchengli_scope
				on_created = {
					#创建时
					save_scope_as = otlchaoxianfanwei_scope					#不能是save_temporary_scope_as，因为后面朝鲜战争要用
					activate_law = law_type:law_council_republic					#委员会共和制
					change_relations = {
						country = c:KOR
						value = -100						#降低和韩国关系
					}
					s:STATE_PYONGYANG = {
						#两西
						random_scope_state = {
							limit = {
								exists = c:JAP
								owner ?= {
									OR = {
										c:JAP ?= this
										is_subject_of = c:JAP
									}
								}
							}
							set_state_owner = scope:otlchaoxianfanwei_scope
						}
					}
					s:STATE_SARIWON = {
						#关北
						random_scope_state = {
							limit = {
								exists = c:JAP
								owner ?= {
									OR = {
										c:JAP ?= this
										is_subject_of = c:JAP
									}
								}
							}
							set_state_owner = scope:otlchaoxianfanwei_scope
						}
					}
				}
			}
			#以下是中国统一
			if = {
				limit = {
					NOT = {
						exists = c:CHI
					}
				}
				s:STATE_NANJING = {
					#南京
					random_scope_state = {
						save_scope_as = rbzb_scope
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:RUS
				}
				s:STATE_OUTER_MANCHURIA = {
					random_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							exists = c:JAP
							owner ?= {
								OR = {
									c:JAP ?= this
									is_subject_of = c:JAP
								}
							}
						}
						set_state_owner = c:RUS
					}
				}
				s:STATE_AMUR = {
					random_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							exists = c:JAP
							owner ?= {
								OR = {
									c:JAP ?= this
									is_subject_of = c:JAP
								}
							}
						}
						set_state_owner = c:RUS
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:SOV
				}
				s:STATE_OUTER_MANCHURIA = {
					random_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							exists = c:JAP
							owner ?= {
								OR = {
									c:JAP ?= this
									is_subject_of = c:JAP
								}
							}
						}
						set_state_owner = c:SOV
					}
				}
				s:STATE_AMUR = {
					random_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							exists = c:JAP
							owner ?= {
								OR = {
									c:JAP ?= this
									is_subject_of = c:JAP
								}
							}
						}
						set_state_owner = c:SOV
					}
				}
			}
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:SOV
					owner ?= {
						OR = {
							c:JAP ?= this
						}
					}
					OR = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_west_siberia_old
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_east_siberia_old
					}
				}
				set_state_owner = c:SOV
			}
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:RUS
					owner ?= {
						OR = {
							c:JAP ?= this
						}
					}
					OR = {
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_west_siberia_old
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_east_siberia_old
					}
				}
				set_state_owner = c:SOV
			}
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:JAP
					owner ?= {
						OR = {
							c:JAP ?= this
						}
					}
					OR = {
						region = sr:region_north_china						#华北
						region = sr:region_south_china						#华南
						is_in_geographic_region = geographic_region_manchuria_old						#满洲
						region = sr:region_central_asia						#中亚
					}
				}
				set_state_owner = c:CHI
			}
			#如果满洲有战败国，也被中国吞并
			if = {
				limit = {
					exists = c:MCH
				}
				c:CHI ?= {
					annex = c:MCH
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					c:USA ?= {
						has_modifier = legendary_modifier
					}
				}
				c:USA ?= {
					create_diplomatic_pact = {
						country = c:KOR						#美国保护韩国
						type = protectorate
					}
				}
				#然后是美国托管琉球和拿日本在太平洋的岛屿
				s:STATE_WEST_MICRONESIA = {
					random_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							exists = c:JAP
							owner ?= {
								OR = {
									c:JAP ?= this
									is_subject_of = c:JAP
								}
							}
						}
						set_state_owner = c:USA
					}
				}
				s:STATE_EAST_MICRONESIA = {
					random_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							exists = c:JAP
							owner ?= {
								OR = {
									c:JAP ?= this
									is_subject_of = c:JAP
								}
							}
						}
						set_state_owner = c:USA
					}
				}
				s:STATE_RYUKYU_ISLANDS = {
					#琉球
					random_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							exists = c:JAP
							owner ?= {
								OR = {
									c:JAP ?= this
									is_subject_of = c:JAP
								}
							}
						}
						set_state_owner = c:USA
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					c:FRA ?= {
						has_modifier = legendary_modifier
					}
				}
				#接下来是法国吃印度支那
				s:STATE_TONKIN = {
					random_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							exists = c:JAP
							owner ?= {
								OR = {
									c:JAP ?= this
									is_subject_of = c:JAP
								}
							}
						}
						set_state_owner = c:FRA
					}
				}
				s:STATE_ANNAM = {
					random_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							exists = c:JAP
							owner ?= {
								OR = {
									c:JAP ?= this
									is_subject_of = c:JAP
								}
							}
						}
						set_state_owner = c:FRA
					}
				}
				s:STATE_LAOS = {
					random_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							exists = c:JAP
							owner ?= {
								OR = {
									c:JAP ?= this
									is_subject_of = c:JAP
								}
							}
						}
						set_state_owner = c:FRA
					}
				}
				s:STATE_MEKONG = {
					random_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							exists = c:JAP
							owner ?= {
								OR = {
									c:JAP ?= this
									is_subject_of = c:JAP
								}
							}
						}
						set_state_owner = c:FRA
					}
				}
				s:STATE_CAMBODIA = {
					random_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							exists = c:JAP
							owner ?= {
								OR = {
									c:JAP ?= this
									is_subject_of = c:JAP
								}
							}
						}
						set_state_owner = c:FRA
					}
				}
			}
			#英国吃印尼和太平洋
			if = {
				limit = {
					c:GBR ?= {
						has_modifier = legendary_modifier
					}
				}
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						exists = c:JAP
						owner ?= {
							OR = {
								c:JAP ?= this
							}
						}
						OR = {
							region = sr:region_indonesia
							region = sr:region_oceania
						}
					}
					set_state_owner = c:GBR
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					c:RUS ?= {
						has_modifier = legendary_modifier
					}
				}
				c:RUS ?= {
					create_diplomatic_pact = {
						country = scope:otlchaoxianfanwei_scope						#苏联保护朝鲜
						type = protectorate
					}
				}
				s:STATE_SAKHALIN = {
					#萨哈林
					random_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							exists = c:JAP
							owner ?= {
								OR = {
									c:JAP ?= this
									is_subject_of = c:JAP
								}
							}
						}
						set_state_owner = c:RUS
					}
				}
				c:JAP ?= {
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_state_in_state_region = STATE_HOKKAIDO
						}
						s:STATE_HOKKAIDO = {
							#北海道
							set_owner_of_provinces = {
								country = c:RUS
								provinces = {
									x601140
									x1E5261
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			if = {
				limit = {
					c:SOV ?= {
						has_modifier = legendary_modifier
					}
				}
				c:SOV ?= {
					create_diplomatic_pact = {
						country = scope:otlchaoxianfanwei_scope						#苏联保护朝鲜
						type = protectorate
					}
				}
				s:STATE_SAKHALIN = {
					#萨哈林
					random_scope_state = {
						limit = {
							exists = c:JAP
							owner ?= {
								OR = {
									c:JAP ?= this
									is_subject_of = c:JAP
								}
							}
						}
						set_state_owner = c:SOV
					}
				}
				c:JAP ?= {
					if = {
						limit = {
							has_state_in_state_region = STATE_HOKKAIDO
						}
						s:STATE_HOKKAIDO = {
							#北海道
