sino_event.235 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = sino_event.235.t
	desc = sino_event.235.d
	flavor = sino_event.235.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_superevent_modern
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/dsk_event_pictures/wearechinese.png"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/state_status_icons/has_devastation.dds"
	trigger = {
		is_player = yes
		NOT = {
			has_variable = 235_var
		}
	}
	cooldown = {
		years = 1000
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = 235_var
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.235.a
		c:JAP ?= {
			add_modifier = {
				name = bad_legendary_modifier
				years = 20
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = alliance_with_jap_var
			}
			add_loyalists = {
				value = 0.1
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = bad_legendary_modifier
				years = 20
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = alliance_with_chi_var
			}
			add_radicals = {
				value = 0.1
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = no_legendary_modifier
				years = 20
				is_decaying = yes
			}
		}
	}
}

#大屠杀
sino_event.236 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = scope:han_state
	event_image = {
		video = "unspecific_fire"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/military_parade"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"
	title = sino_event.236.t
	desc = sino_event.236.d
	flavor = sino_event.236.f
	cooldown = {
		years = 1
	}
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	minor_left_icon = c:CHI
	minor_right_icon = c:JAP
	trigger = {
		has_war_with = c:CHI
		c:CHI ?= {
			any_scope_state = {
				devastation > 0.1
				is_incorporated = yes
			}
		}
		any_interest_group = {
			OR = {
				leader = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_meiji
				}
				leader = {
					has_ideology = ideology:ideology_fascist
				}
			}
			is_in_government = yes
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_ideology = ideology:ideology_meiji
				is_in_government = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = empire_character
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_ideology = ideology:ideology_fascist
				is_in_government = yes
			}
			save_scope_as = fascist_character
		}
		c:CHI ?= {
			save_scope_as = chi_scope
			random_scope_state = {
				limit = {
					devastation > 0.1
					is_incorporated = yes
				}
				save_scope_as = han_state
			}
		}
		c:JAP ?= {
			save_scope_as = scope_yolo
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.236.a
		custom_tooltip = no_fascist_character
		change_infamy = 10
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:empire_character = {
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = a_war_criminal
						has_modifier = b_war_criminal
						has_modifier = c_war_criminal
					}
				}
			}
			scope:empire_character = {
				add_modifier = c_war_criminal
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:empire_character = {
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = a_war_criminal
						has_modifier = b_war_criminal
					}
					has_modifier = c_war_criminal
				}
			}
			scope:empire_character = {
				remove_modifier = c_war_criminal
				add_modifier = b_war_criminal
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:empire_character = {
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = a_war_criminal
						has_modifier = c_war_criminal
					}
					has_modifier = b_war_criminal
				}
			}
			scope:empire_character = {
				remove_modifier = b_war_criminal
				add_modifier = a_war_criminal
			}
		}
		#
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:fascist_character = {
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = a_war_criminal
						has_modifier = b_war_criminal
						has_modifier = c_war_criminal
					}
				}
			}
			scope:fascist_character = {
				add_modifier = c_war_criminal
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:fascist_character = {
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = a_war_criminal
						has_modifier = b_war_criminal
					}
					has_modifier = c_war_criminal
				}
			}
			scope:fascist_character = {
				remove_modifier = c_war_criminal
				add_modifier = b_war_criminal
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				scope:fascist_character = {
					NOT = {
						has_modifier = a_war_criminal
						has_modifier = c_war_criminal
					}
					has_modifier = b_war_criminal
				}
			}
			scope:fascist_character = {
				remove_modifier = b_war_criminal
				add_modifier = a_war_criminal
			}
		}
		#全员甲级战犯
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					scope:fascist_character = {
						has_modifier = a_war_criminal
					}
					scope:empire_character = {
						has_modifier = a_war_criminal
					}
				}
			}
			ruler ?= {
				add_modifier = a_war_criminal
			}
		}
		scope:han_state = {
			state_region = {
				add_devastation = 50
			}
			kill_population_percent_in_state = {
				percent = 0.1
				culture = cu:han
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = sub_china_war_bar
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					has_journal_entry = je_protect_china
				}
				je:je_protect_china = {
					add_progress = {
						value = 1
						name = je_protect_china_war_bar
					}
				}
				je:je_protect_china = {
					add_progress = {
						value = -20
						name = je_china_war_bar
					}
				}
			}
		}
		custom_tooltip = {
			text = add_protect_china_war_bar_tt
			every_country = {
				limit = {
					is_ai = yes
					OR = {
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_north_china
						has_interest_marker_in_region = sr:region_south_china
					}
					NOT = {
						c:CHI ?= this
					}
					NOT = {
						c:JAP ?= this
					}
				}
				create_diplomatic_catalyst = {
					type = catalyst_event_negative
					target = root
				}
			}
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					is_player = yes
				}
			}
			post_notification = slaughter_human
		}
	}
}

#毒气战
sino_event.238 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = war_crimes.1.t
	desc = war_crimes.1.d
	flavor = war_crimes.1.f
	event_image = {
		texture = "gfx/event_pictures/dsk_event_pictures/ita2.png"
	}
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/bicorne_hat.dds"
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	minor_left_icon = c:CHI
	minor_right_icon = c:JAP
	trigger = {
		has_war_with = c:CHI
		has_technology_researched = chemical_warfare		#化学战
		any_scope_general = {
			#任意将领配备毒气
			owner = ROOT
			this.commander_military_formation = {
				has_mobilization_option = mobilization_option:mobilization_option_chemical_weapons
			}
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		random_scope_war = {
			limit = {
				any_scope_front = {
					any_scope_general = {
						owner = ROOT
						this.commander_military_formation = {
							has_mobilization_option = mobilization_option:mobilization_option_chemical_weapons
						}
					}
					any_scope_state = {
						NOT = {
							owner = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
			}
			random_scope_front = {
				limit = {
					any_scope_general = {
						owner = ROOT
						this.commander_military_formation = {
							has_mobilization_option = mobilization_option:mobilization_option_chemical_weapons
						}
					}
					any_scope_state = {
						NOT = {
							owner = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				random_scope_general = {
					limit = {
						owner = ROOT
						this.commander_military_formation = {
							has_mobilization_option = mobilization_option:mobilization_option_chemical_weapons
						}
					}
					save_scope_as = war_crimes_perpetrator
				}
				random_scope_state = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							owner = ROOT
						}
					}
					save_scope_as = war_crimes_state
				}
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = war_crimes.1.a
		default_option = yes
		scope:war_crimes_perpetrator = {
			add_trait = war_criminal
		}
		scope:war_crimes_state = {
			add_modifier = {
				name = mustard_gas
				days = normal_modifier_time
				multiplier = 2
			}
		}
		change_infamy = 5
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					infamy < 94
				}
				add = 100
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = clever_dsk.30.b
		change_infamy = -2
		ai_chance = {
			base = 1
			modifier = {
				trigger = {
					infamy > 94
				}
				add = 100
			}
		}
	}
}

sino_event.239 = {
	#隐藏事件
	type = country_event
	hidden = yes
	cooldown = {
		days = 180
	}
	trigger = {
		c:JAP ?= this
		c:JAP ?= {
			infamy >= 100
		}
		c:CHI ?= {
			has_war_with = c:JAP
		}
		NOT = {
			has_variable = china_protected_var
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = {
			name = china_protected_var
			days = 180
		}
		every_country = {
			limit = {
				has_journal_entry = je_protect_china
			}
			custom_tooltip = {
				text = add_protect_china_war_bar_tt
				je:je_protect_china = {
					add_progress = {
						value = 1
						name = je_protect_china_war_bar
					}

sino_event.241 = {
	#东京审判
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	event_image = {
		video = "usa2"
	}
	gui_window = event_window_superevent_modern
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/roc1.dds"
	title = sino_event.241.t
	desc = sino_event.241.d
	flavor = sino_event.241.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	minor_left_icon = c:CHI
	minor_right_icon = c:USA
	trigger = {
		is_player = yes
		OR = {
			c:CHI ?= this
			has_variable = alliance_with_chi_var
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		c:USA ?= {
			save_scope_as = usa_scope
		}
		c:JAP ?= {
			save_scope_as = jap_scope
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = a_war_criminal
				}
				save_scope_as = empire_character
			}
			random_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_ideology = a_war_criminal
					NOT = {
						this = scope:empire_character
					}
				}
				save_scope_as = fascist_character
			}
		}
		c:GBR ?= {
			save_scope_as = gbr_scope
		}
		c:CHI ?= {
			save_scope_as = chi_scope
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.241.a
		c:JAP ?= {
			activate_law = law_type:law_IJ_secularization
			activate_law = law_type:law_universal_suffrage
			activate_law = law_type:law_national_supremacy
			every_scope_character = {
				limit = {
					has_modifier = a_war_criminal
					is_ruler_of_own_country = no
				}
				kill_character = yes
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				c:JAP ?= {
					is_player = no
					ruler ?= {
						has_modifier = a_war_criminal
					}
				}
			}
			random_list = {
				50 = {
					trigger_event = {
						id = sino_event.242
						days = 30
					}
				}
				50 = {
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

		}

		}
		c:GBR ?= {

			save_scope_as = chi_scope
		}

	option = {

		if = {
			limit = {
				NOT = {
					c:USA ?= this
				}
			}
			c:USA ?= {
				change_relations = {
					country = root
					value = -100
				}
			}
		}
		c:JAP ?= {
			change_infamy = -100
			change_relations = {
				country = root
				value = -100
			}
			add_radicals = {
				value = 0.1
			}
			ruler ?= {
				kill_character = yes
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			50 = {
				c:JAP ?= {
					activate_law = law_type:law_presidential_republic
				}
			}
			50 = {
			}
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.242.b
		custom_tooltip = historical_tt
		default_option = yes
		add_modifier = {
			name = add_jap_war_reparations
			years = 15
		}
		c:JAP ?= {
			change_relations = {
				country = root
				value = 100
			}
			add_modifier = {
				name = jap_war_reparations
				years = 15
			}
			ruler ?= {
				remove_modifier = a_war_criminal
			}
		}
	}
}

sino_event.260 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = root
	title = sino_event.260.t
	desc = sino_event.260.d
	flavor = sino_event.260.f
	duration = 3
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:crisis_scope_2
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/unspecific/asia_union_leader"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_skull.dds"
	cooldown = {
		years = 100
	}
	trigger = {
		exists = c:JAP
		root = c:JAP
		has_law_or_variant = law_type:law_monarchy
		NOT = {
			has_variable = is_empiredie_ninko
		}
		game_date > 1845.12.26
		any_scope_character = {
			has_template = JAP_ninko_yamato
		}
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = {
			name = is_empiredie_ninko
			value = yes
		}
		ruler ?= {
			set_variable = {
				name = ecchi_27_shengbi
			}
			save_scope_as = crisis_scope_2
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.260.a
		default_option = yes
		s:STATE_KANTO = {
			start_harvest_condition = moderate_rainfall
		}
		every_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_template = JAP_ninko_yamato
			}
			kill_character = yes
		}
	}
}

#触发旅行
sino_event.264 = {
	type = country_event
	placement = ROOT
	title = sino_event.264.t
	desc = sino_event.264.d
	flavor = sino_event.264.f
	gui_window = event_window_1char_tabloid
	left_icon = scope:change_your_animations_now_character
	on_created_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/UI/Alerts/event_appear"
	on_opened_soundeffect = "event:/SFX/Events/misc/1Character_Banner"
	icon = "gfx/interface/icons/event_icons/event_portrait.dds"
	duration = 1
	dlc = dlc_ra_plus
	trigger = {
		# triggered by marry_ruler_or_heir interaction
	}
	immediate = {
		set_variable = {
			name = dsk_go_travel_now
			years = 10
		}
		random_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = change_your_animations_now_three
			}
			save_scope_as = change_your_animations_now_character	
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.264.a
		default_option = yes
		custom_tooltip = dsk_be_traveller
		scope:change_your_animations_now_character = {
			set_variable = dsk_go_travel
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			scope:change_your_animations_now_character = {
				remove_variable = change_your_animations_now_three
			}
		}
		add_journal_entry = {
			type = je_green_magical_girl_world_tour
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 0
		}
	}
	option = {
		name = sino_event.264.b
		every_scope_character = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = dsk_go_travel
			}
			remove_variable = dsk_go_travel
		}
		hidden_effect = {
			scope:change_your_animations_now_character = {
				remove_variable = change_your_animations_now_three
			}
		}
		ai_chance = {
			base = 100
		}
	}
}
